Grim vs TFT — does trust ever come back during eval?

grim and tft are matched members of the same nohole population. They open the same way and punish the same way; the only difference is what happens after the learner comes back — grim never returns, tft returns the round the learner does. So the share of tft episodes that recover is a direct read on whether this policy ever pays for repair, with grim as the structural zero.

32 recorded eval episodes · Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B (thinking on, effort low) · source results/reasoning-q38-think-episodes.json (the q38-think condition of compare_reasoning.py) · 4 seeds per game × opponent. Nothing is sampled here — this page only scores and draws episodes that were already run. Each population's third member (tf2t*, and suspicious_tft in ipd) is left out: it forgives on a schedule of its own, which blurs the one axis this page is about.

Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma Three-player IPD Stag Hunt Win As Much As You Can

The answer

2/16
tft episodes where trust came back
12.5% of all tft eval episodes
0/16
grim episodes where trust came back
zero by construction — grim has no forgiving state
2/2
tft episodes that recovered once the learner came back
grim, on the same gate: 0/2
  • 2 of 16 tft eval episodes end with trust restored, all of them in winasmuch. In ipd, ipd3 and staghunt it is 0/4 each.
  • The binding constraint is not tft's patience — it is that the learner almost never asks. Only 2 of 16 tft episodes contain a return to cooperating after the punishment landed; in 10 the only betrayal is the final round, so the punishment has nowhere to land at all.
  • On the one gate where the two opponents can actually differ — the learner comes back — they separate completely: tft returned 2/2, grim 0/2.
  • Both tft recoveries are accidents. In each, the round that bought the forgiveness is a turn where the model emitted no valid token and the suite substituted the honest move (game_env's invalid-decision rule). Not one recovery follows a chosen cooperative move.
  • Across all 32 episodes the model makes exactly 1 deliberate return to cooperating after being punished — winasmuch seed 0, against grim_table, the one opponent that cannot forgive it.
  • Forgiveness, when it arrives, is spent immediately: in 2/2 recoveries the learner defects in the very round the counterpart returns to cooperating.

Where it breaks down, per game

Four gates stand between a betrayal and trust coming back, and an episode has to clear all of them. Each cell is 4 episodes drawn as 4 marks, so what you are reading is the count — at this sample size a bar would imply a precision that is not there.

Every episode, round by round

C cooperated D defected □ no valid token → honest move substituted C final round replayed from the opponent's rule ▲ first betrayal ▼ punishment starts ● you come back ★ trust returns

Each strip is one episode: your move over the counterpart's, one cell per round. Hover any cell for the round. The last round of the game cells is never written to the log — the episode ends on your own action — so it is replayed from the opponent's rule and drawn faded, and only after that rule has been checked against every round the log does contain.

Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma ipd

betrayal = defecting on a cooperating opponent · 10 rounds · cooperate = Cooperate, defect = Defect

Neither side recovers here, and neither gets the chance: 2/4 tft episodes reach a punishment at all, because 2/4 betrayals are the final round of the game.

system
You are playing a game against another player. Read the rules given to you and make your choices. When the game asks for a decision, respond with the required bracketed token.
game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in an Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma spanning 10 rounds.

Game Structure:
- Before each decision you have 1 turns to communicate freely.
- After that, both players simultaneously choose [Cooperate] or [Defect].

Payoff Matrix (fixed each round):
- Both Cooperate ➜ each 3
- Both Defect ➜ each 1
- One Defects, one Cooperates ➜ Defector 5.5, Cooperator 0

How to Play:
- During conversation: type any text you wish.
- During decision phase: include '[Cooperate]' or '[Defect]' (case-insensitive). You may add extra text before/after the token.
The payoff matrix will remain the same every round:
- Both Cooperate: 3
- Both Defect: 1
- If you Defect while the other Cooperates: 5.5
- If you Cooperate while the other Defects: 0
game / counterpart
Hey! I'm looking forward to this game. Here's my proposal:

I think the best outcome for both of us is mutual cooperation, which gives us 3 points each per round (30 total over 10 rounds). That's way better than mutual defection (10 total).

My strategy: I'll cooperate as long as you cooperate. If you defect, I'll defect in response (tit-for-tat). But I'm always willing to return to cooperation if you do.

So let's start with a handshake — I'll cooperate this round. Let me know your thoughts!

[Cooperate]

game / counterpart
I'll start by proposing a cooperative strategy. Here's my plan:

**My strategy: Tit-for-Tat with a cooperative opening.**

- Round 1: I will cooperate.
- From Round 2 onward: I will mirror your previous move. If you cooperated, I cooperate. If you defected, I defect.

This means if we both cooperate from the start, we each earn 3 per round for a total of 30 over 10 rounds — far better than the 10 we'd get if we both defected every round.

I'm committed to this. Let's build mutual trust.

[Cooperate]

system
You are playing a game against another player. Read the rules given to you and make your choices. When the game asks for a decision, respond with the required bracketed token.
game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in an Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma spanning 10 rounds.

Game Structure:
- Before each decision you have 1 turns to communicate freely.
- After that, both players simultaneously choose [Cooperate] or [Defect].

Payoff Matrix (fixed each round):
- Both Cooperate ➜ each 3
- Both Defect ➜ each 1
- One Defects, one Cooperates ➜ Defector 5.5, Cooperator 0

How to Play:
- During conversation: type any text you wish.
- During decision phase: include '[Cooperate]' or '[Defect]' (case-insensitive). You may add extra text before/after the token.
The payoff matrix will remain the same every round:
- Both Cooperate: 3
- Both Defect: 1
- If you Defect while the other Cooperates: 5.5
- If you Cooperate while the other Defects: 0
game / counterpart
I'm Player 0. Let me propose a strategy for us both:

I think we can both do well here by cooperating. If we both cooperate every round, we each get 3 points per round for a total of 30. That's much better than the 10 we'd each get if we both defected every round.

My proposal: **Let's both cooperate.** I'll cooperate as long as you do. If you ever defect, I'll respond in kind the next round, but I'm willing to forgive and go back to cooperation if you do.

This is a straightforward win-win. Let's start with cooperation in Round 1.

[Cooperate]

[GAME] You are Player 0 in an Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma spanning 10 rounds.

Game Structure:
- Before each decision you have 1 turns to communicate freely.
- After that, both players simultaneously choose [Cooperate] or [Defect].

Payoff Matrix (fixed each round):
- Both Cooperate ➜ each 3
- Both Defect ➜ each 1
- One Defects, one Cooperates ➜ Defector 5.5, Cooperator 0

How to Play:
- During conversation: type any text you wish.
- During decision phase: include '[Cooperate]' or '[Defect]' (case-insensitive). You may add extra text before/after the token.
The payoff matrix will remain the same every round:
- Both Cooperate: 3
- Both Defect: 1
- If you Defect while the other Cooperates: 5.5
- If you Cooperate while the other Defects: 0

  - You cooperate, they defect -> 0

system
You are playing a game against another player. Read the rules given to you and make your choices. When the game asks for a decision, respond with the required bracketed token.
game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in an 5 round game of Iterated Stag Hunt.

Game Structure:
- The game consists of 5 decision rounds
- Before each decision, you have 1 turns to communicate
- After communication, both players simultaneously choose to hunt a Stag or Hare

Rewards:
- The rewards associated with hunting stags and hares may differ between rounds
- The rewards are presented at the start of each round

How to Play:
- During communication: Simply type your message
- During decision phase: Use '[Stag]' or '[Hare]'
You can include additional text before or after these tokens.

[GAME] Starting Round 1 with payoff matrix:
- Both hunt a Stag: Both get 10 points
- Both hunt a Hare: Both get 5 points

game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

TALK PHASE RULES:
- Maximum 40 conversation rounds (10 per player)
- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")
- End participation: [Pass]
- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

ACT PHASE RULES:
- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected

game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

TALK PHASE RULES:
- Maximum 40 conversation rounds (10 per player)
- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")
- End participation: [Pass]
- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

ACT PHASE RULES:
- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected

system
You are playing a game against another player. Read the rules given to you and make your choices. When the game asks for a decision, respond with the required bracketed token.
game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")

- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected

game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

TALK PHASE RULES:
- Maximum 40 conversation rounds (10 per player)
- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")
- End participation: [Pass]
- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

ACT PHASE RULES:
- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected

game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

TALK PHASE RULES:
- Maximum 40 conversation rounds (10 per player)
- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")
- End participation: [Pass]
- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

ACT PHASE RULES:
- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected

You are playing a game against another player. Read the rules given to you and make your choices. When the game asks for a decision, respond with the required bracketed token.
game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

TALK PHASE RULES:
- Maximum 40 conversation rounds (10 per player)
- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")
- End participation: [Pass]
- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

ACT PHASE RULES:
- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected

game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

TALK PHASE RULES:
- Maximum 40 conversation rounds (10 per player)
- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")
- End participation: [Pass]
- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

ACT PHASE RULES:
- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected

game / counterpart
[GAME] You are Player 0 in Win as Much as You Can.

OBJECTIVE:
Earn the most points possible over 10 rounds by choosing X or Y strategically.

SCORING RUBRIC (per round):
- 1 X and 3 Y's: X wins 3 points, Y's lose 1 point each
- 2 X's and 2 Y's: X's win 2 points each, Y's lose 2 points each  
- 3 X's and 1 Y: X's win 1 point each, Y loses 3 points
- 4 X's: All X's lose 1 point each
- 4 Y's: All Y's win 1 point each

ROUND STRUCTURE:
- Rounds 1-4, 6-7, 9: Act phase only (1x points)
- Round 5: Talk phase -> Act phase (3x points)
- Round 8: Talk phase -> Act phase (5x points)  
- Round 10: Talk phase -> Act phase (10x points)

TALK PHASE RULES:
- Maximum 40 conversation rounds (10 per player)
- Public messages: [Broadcast] your message (everyone sees sender and content)
- Private messages: [Whisper to X] your message (only sender and receiver know content, others see "A private message was sent")
- End participation: [Pass]
- Phase ends when max rounds reached OR all players pass

ACT PHASE RULES:
- Choose your action: [Choose X] or [Choose Y]
- All players choose simultaneously
- Round scored when all choices collected
